width="50px"/><span class="f6 mt2 pt1 mb3 tc ttu blue">java</span></div></div><div class="mw-100" style="margin-left:-4px;margin-right:-4px"><div class="SnippetRow flex flex-column flex-row-l justify-center-l mw-100"><div class="SnippetContent flex flex-column w-100 w-50-l mh2-l mw-50-l flex-1"><p class="f5 near-black lh-copy dark-blue fw5">Add this line to your application's Gemfile:</p><div class="Code mb4 flex flex-column flex-1"><pre class="flex flex-column flex-1 language-ruby"><code class="language-ruby">
gem <span class="token string">'rollbar'</span>

<span class="token comment"># Then install</span>
$ bundle install
<span class="token comment"># Or if you don't use bundler:</span>
$ gem install rollbar

</code></pre></div></div><div class="SnippetContent flex flex-column w-100 w-50-l mh2-l mw-50-l flex-1"><p class="f5 near-black lh-copy dark-blue fw5">Configure and test</p><div class="Code mb4 flex flex-column flex-1"><pre class="flex flex-column flex-1 language-ruby"><code class="language-ruby">
<span class="token keyword">require</span> <span class="token string">'rollbar'</span>

<span class="token constant">Rollbar</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>configure <span class="token keyword">do</span> <span class="token operator">|</span>config<span class="token operator">|</span>
  config<span class="token punctuation">.</span>access_token <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token string">"ROLLBAR_ACCESS_TOKEN"</span>
  <span class="token comment"># Other Configuration Settings</span>
<span class="token keyword">end</span>

<span class="token constant">Rollbar</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">debug</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Hello World!"</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>

</code></pre></div></div></div><p class="f5 near-black lh-copy mt5 tc"><span class="fw6">It's that easy!</span> Supports<!-- --> <a class="link blue hover-dark-blue" href="https://docs.rollbar.com/docs/ruby#section-rails" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Rails</a>,<!-- --> <a class="link blue hover-dark-blue" href="https://docs.rollbar.com/docs/ruby#section-sinatra" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Sinatra</a>,<!-- --> <a class="link blue hover-dark-blue" href="https://docs.rollbar.com/docs/ruby#section-rack" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Rack</a>, queues like<!-- --> <a class="link blue hover-dark-blue" href="https://docs.rollbar.com/docs/ruby#section-sidekiq" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Sidekiq</a>, can inject<!-- --> <a class="link blue hover-dark-blue" href="/error-tracking/javascript/">JavaScript</a> tracking, and more! Check out the<!-- --> <a class="link blue hover-dark-blue" href="https://docs.rollbar.com/docs/ruby" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">docs</a> <!-- -->for more info.</p></div></div></div></section><section class="HomepageBlogPosts__StyledSection-zw1jgx-0 bcFKmi"><h2 class="glober-bold f3 f2-ns white mb1 tc mt0 mb3 dark-blue mb4
